[EVENT: IRN23613]
Date: 2023-07-01
Country: Iran | Location: Zabol
Type: Violence against civilians > Attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Police Forces of Iran (1989-) Islamic Republic of Iran Border Guard Command [State forces]
Actor2: Civilians (Afghanistan) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 4
Sources: BBC News; Haalvash; Radio Zamaneh; Tasnim News Agency [Other-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 July 2023, Iran Border Guard Command opened fire at 5 Afghanistani citizens during illegal crossing the Nimruz border of Iran and Afghanistan (coded to Zabol city) (Nimrouz, Sistan and Baluchestan) and killed 4 of them and another was wounded. The Iranian government has denied this claim.

---

DESCRIPTION:

The rallies after Friday prayer have been organized continuously in the past months following the beginning of Mahsa Amini's movement.

On 21 July 2023, military Forces, likely Islamic Revolutionary Guard Corps, assaulted and beat with batons and electric shockers a group of eco-tourist hikers in Opert village near Shahmirzad city (Semnan), because they believed their activities were disrespectful to Shiite Muslim beliefs as they took place during Muharram. Several were injured and hospitalized.

[EVENT: IRN23806]

Actor1: Rioters (Iran) [Rioters]
Actor2: Police Forces of Iran (1989-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: BBC News; Ensaf News; Eteraze Bazar; Hengaw; Human Rights Activists in Iran (HRA); Kurdistan Human Rights Network; Radio Zamaneh; VOA [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 30 July 2023, local job seekers from Aqdareh village held a demonstration and blocked the entrance of the Travertine mine near Takab city (East Azerbaijan) to demand the recruitment of local youth in the mine. Demonstrators got involved in physical clashes with mine guards; as a result, a demonstrator and a mine guard were injured and transferred to the hospital. The demonstration continued until then early morning of the next day, when the police raided the village and shot civilians and arrested about 50 demonstrators. As a result, 3 demonstrators were injured and taken to medical centers. Houses and cars were also damaged during the clashes.

[EVENT: IRN23821]
Date: 2023-08-02
Country: Iran | Location: Abu Musa
Type: Strategic developments > Change to group/activity | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Military Forces of Iran (1989-) Islamic Revolutionary Guard Corps [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: BBC News; Tasnim News Agency; VOA [National-International]

DESCRIPTION:
Movement of forces: On 2 August 2023, large numbers of navy IRGC forces deployed into the coast of Sirri, Greater Tunb, Lesser Tunb, and Abu Musa islands (coded to Abu Musa city) (Hormozgan) for the Eqtedar naval exercise. Presumably, this naval exercise was in response to the US deploying a destroyer and fighter aircraft to the Persian Gulf and also over the final statement of the sixth round of strategic dialogues between the foreign minister of Russia and the member states of the Persian Gulf Cooperation Council concerning the three Iranian islands. The Commander-in-Chief and Commander of the IRGC Navy went to Abu Musa island for this naval exercise, and IRGC unveiled the vessels equipped with missiles of a 600 km range.

[EVENT: IRN23973]
Date: 2023-08-13
Country: Iran | Location: Shiraz
Type: Violence against civilians > Attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Islamic State Khorasan Province (ISKP) [Rebel group]
Actor2: Civilians (Iran) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 4
Sources: BBC News; FARS News Agency (Iran); Human Rights Activists in Iran (HRA); Tasnim News Agency; VOA [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 13 August 2023, a militant from Tajikistan and likely a member of the Islamic State, attacked the Shah Cheragh Shrine in Shiraz city (Fars). The attacker entered the Shah Cheragh shrine with a machine gun from Bab al-Mahdi's entrance and immediately started shooting at the pilgrims. As a result, 4 pilgrims were killed, and 8 others were wounded. The attacker was arrested by the security forces at the scene. The Chief Justice of Fars province announced that 20 suspects, at least 8 of them have foreign identities were arrested in connection with this attack, and the Ministry of Intelligence claimed that the attacker was a member of the Islamic State. Shah Cheragh Shrine is the third most important shrine in Shia Islam.

[EVENT: IRN23975]
Date: 2023-08-18
Country: Iran | Location: Rasht
Type: Riots > Mob violence | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Rioters (Iran) [Rioters]
Actor2: Rioters (Iran) [Rioters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Human Rights Activists in Iran (HRA) [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 18 August 2023, a warning about the observance of hijab by a religious member of the 'enjoining good and forbidding wrong' caused a physical clash between a women rioter and 4 rioters in Rasht city (Gilan). After the clash, the police arrested the 4 rioters. After the Mahsa movement, the hijab question has become a sociopolitical matter and the religious leaders who are supported by the government and the police, try to prevent any anti-veil activities by Iranian women. State violence against and arresting of women without hijab has led to demonstrations in the past.

Sources: BBC News; Human Rights Activists in Iran (HRA) [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
Other: On 21 August 2023, a warning about the observance of hijab by a religious member of 'enjoining good and forbidding wrong' caused a physical clash with a woman who was riding a motorcycle in Tehran city (Tehran). After the clash, the police arrested her. After the Mahsa movement, the hijab question has become a sociopolitical matter, and the religious leaders whom the government and the police support daily try to prevent anti-veil activities by Iranian women. State violence against and arresting of women without hijab has led to protests in the past.

[EVENT: IRN23912]
Date: 2023-08-23
Country: Iran | Location: Izeh
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Police Forces of Iran (1989-) [State forces]
Actor2: Unidentified Armed Group (Iran) [Political militia]
Fatalities: 2
Sources: 1500 Tasvir; BBC News; Iran International; Mehr News Agency; Rokna; Tasnim News Agency; Vahid Online; VOA [New media-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 23 August 2023, police stormed the gunmen's house and an armed clash erupted in Izeh (Khuzestan). Police forces opened fire and as a result, 2 young men were killed and 3 others were arrested. Police claimed that the operation was set to arrest the killers of the Journalist Akbar Limuchi who died last month in Izeh and it was an operation combating drug traffickers. Press agencies outside Iran reported that the operation was against political opponents of the government and it is related to last year's demonstrations and Mahsa Amini's movement.

The police beat and arrested four protesters while they were on their way home, but did not otherwise intervene.
